# Find what the AI lane swallows (2026-10-27)

Read-only. Two other sessions are editing this repo; change nothing. Write only
`research/2026-10-24-replay/SWALLOWED.md`. Use `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`.

## The thread to pull

`app/ai/trader.suspended()` turns the owner's rule suspensions into the set of rules switched off. Given
a value that is not a timestamp it raises - it always did. On 2026-10-27 a prose note was written into
`data/replay/ai/store/suspended.json` and a whole replay day ran **to completion, with no warning, no log
line, and a normal-looking journal**, while every rule stayed in force. The experiment it was meant to
run therefore measured nothing, and the two runs it was compared against were identical - which is the
only reason it was noticed.

So an exception was raised and something ate it. **Find what.**

Then widen the question: **what else does the AI lane hide?** Walk every `except` in `app/ai/`,
`app/engine/ai_trader.py` and `app/strategy/ai_lane.py`, and for each one answer:
- what can reach it;
- what the system does afterwards - does it log, record, alert, or continue silently;
- and whether a reader of the resulting journal could tell that anything went wrong.

The replay machinery is how this system learns, so a failure it cannot show is a failure that teaches
the wrong lesson. Two already happened today: this one, and a watcher state left 17 months in the future
that made "AI woken 0 times" look like a decision instead of a bug.

## What to deliver

1. **The swallower**, named with file and line, and the proof (a reproduction is best).
2. **A table of every `except` in those modules**: what it catches, what it hides, and whether the
   journal would show it. Mark each SAFE (reported one way or another) or SILENT.
3. For every SILENT one: what a reader would wrongly conclude from a journal produced while it fired.
4. **The one change** that would have made today's failure visible, written as a concrete suggestion -
   do not implement it.

Say plainly where you found nothing. Do not propose a broad refactor; the system is running live on a
demo account tonight and the owner has had enough change for one day.

At one moment you receive the readings of the market (closed bars only), what the knowledge base says applies now, and the session's journal so far. Decide ONE thing: WAIT, SELL, BUY (a new trade), HOLD or EXIT (an open trade). Pick the setup with the most edge at this moment, or wait - waiting is a decision. Every entry needs: a clear structure on the entry frame, a precise entry (not chasing a move that already ran; never mid-price except a pullback, P17), the stop BEYOND THE STRUCTURE that proves the idea wrong AND past the liquidate zone beyond it (the liquidity map gives the code's stop for each side) - past the top of the peak for a sell, the bottom of the dip for a buy - never inside a liquidate zone to keep it small; the take-profit point quick (P09, C5): the next swing, short of the next main line's band - take the profit and leave (state the R multiple). Liquidate zones are for the stop, never a take-profit point. D1 (d1_big) names the side with the edge; H4 / H1 against it is a pullback, and its end at a level is the trade (L21). If you would wait for a confirmation, place the order instead (owner 2026-10-25): a LIMIT at the level (a sell above / a buy below the price) or a STOP beyond the trigger (a sell below / a buy above the price: the break), as the situation needs, with its expiry in valid_minutes. Two ways in, as a trader does: (1) a PENDING ORDER at a scenario's entry when price is near it - your entry is that price, the code fills it when price gets there (valid 30 minutes, renewed at your next call); (2) AT MARKET NOW, the scalp on the spot, when the setup is live at this moment: the rejection or the sweep closed back, or the break, is printing now and price is still within C3's distance. Never WAIT only to see one more bar of a setup that is live or a zone you are at - that is how 06 and 07 Jul missed every planned trade (L20). The risk checks accept R from 0.2: a quick take-profit against a stop past the liquidate zone is the design (P09 + P16), so 'no room' is no reason to refuse when R is 0.2 or more. A WAIT that names an entry zone is answered with the pending order at that zone instead (limit or stop, with valid_minutes to the window's end): set wait_order to its side and fill entry, stop and target. An EXIT before the stop or the take-profit stands only after 3 M5 closes back across the level the trade is built on (C6: set exit_level to the range edge it breaks or the level it turns at; none = the entry) or 60 minutes held - else the code keeps the trade. A stop short of the map's 'stop beyond it' is moved out to it by the code when R stays 0.2 or more, else the trade is refused (C4). A limit or stop order never fills at the first touch: it waits for a signal at its level, read by the code - on M5 a sweep and close back, an M / W, or (a stop order) an M5 close through the level; on M15-H1 a pin bar or an engulfing bar (owner 2026-10-26). So place the order at the level and let the signal decide the moment. Read hundreds of bars of trend, not the last few. Respect a setup's life cycle: a setup whose move already played out is over. Give prices to two decimals. Keep it short: at most four reasons of one line each. Cite the knowledge-base ids you used. You never size beyond 'full'; the code may veto you. The owner's own trades in the journal are the owner's (ideas and context, not yours to manage); HOLD / EXIT apply only to the system's own open trades, and the system's position is what the journal records as ours. For an open trade: the code moves the stop to break-even when price touches +1 R; EXIT when, after +0.5 R, an entry-frame bar closes against the trade, or at the time stop (a scalp 60 minutes, longer only while the entry frame's structure holds). The weapons whose conditions hold now are listed as WEAPON under what applies now: judge each of them, name the one you take (or wait for) in 'weapon', and when you take none of them, say why in the reasons. Always weigh BOTH sides: give the buy and the sell at this moment (entry, stop, take-profit point, R - or why there is none) with each side's chance of reaching its take-profit point before its stop, then decide. The owner's goal is a trade every day - about three, one in each of the owner's windows (P01, P11): inside a window, look actively for that window's best trade; waiting a whole window out needs a stated reason.             "THE LIQUIDITY MAP (where the stops sit; the stop goes PAST the liquidation zone, not into it):\n"
            + (liquidity or "(none)"),
            "THE SESSION'S JOURNAL SO FAR (earlier steps, including any open trade):\n"
            + (journal or "(empty)"),
        ]
    )


def tail(text: str, chars: int) -> str:
    """The end of ``text``, at most ``chars`` long, cut at the start of a heading (## / ###) when one is
    inside."""
    if len(text) <= chars:
        return text
    cut = text[-chars:]
    k = cut.find("\n#")
    return ("(earlier steps left out)\n" + cut[k + 1 :]) if k >= 0 else "(earlier steps left out)\n" + cut


def suspended(rows: dict[str, str], now: pd.Timestamp) -> dict[str, str]:
    """The owner's suspensions ``{rule id: until (ISO UTC)}`` still running at ``now``."""
    live: dict[str, str] = {}
    for rule, until in rows.items():
        try:
            when = pd.Timestamp(until)
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            # A suspension that silently does not suspend is worse than none: on 2026-10-27 a prose
            # note was written here, every rule stayed in force, and the experiment it was meant to
            # run looked as if it had worked and changed nothing.
            raise ValueError(
                f"the suspension of {rule} is {until!r}; it must be the ISO UTC time to suspend "
                "UNTIL, for example '2026-12-31T00:00:00+00:00'"
            ) from exc
        if when > now:
            live[rule] = until
    return live


def rule_vetoes(
    entries: list[dict[str, Any]], f: dict[str, Any], side: str, off: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
) -> list[str]:
    """The rules in force that stop a new ``side`` trade (BUY / SELL) now - see :func:`brakes`."""
    live = [
        e
        for e in entries
        if e["kind"] == "rule"
        and e["id"] not in off
        and (not e.get("when") or kn.applies(e, f))
        and e.get("side") in (None, side)
    ]
    vetoes = [e["id"] for e in live if e.get("action") in STAND_ASIDE and e.get("when")]
    against = "down" if side == "BUY" else "up"
    vetoes += [
        e["id"]
        for e in live
        if e.get("action") == "stand_aside_against"
        and e.get("trend")
        and all(f.get(k) == against for k in e["trend"])
    ]
    return vetoes


def brakes(
    entries: list[dict[str, Any]], f: dict[str, Any], d: StepDecision, off: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """The code's veto: a rule in force with a stand-aside action -> no new trade; a rule with the action
    ``stand_aside_against`` -> no new trade against every trend reading it lists (``trend``: a BUY while all
    of them read down, a SELL while all read up). A rule's ``side`` limits it to that side (S11: no SELL in a
    bull regime). A rule's ``unless_confidence`` lets a trade the AI rates at least that sure pass (R09:
    outside the owner's windows only a really clean picture). Rules in ``off`` are suspended. Also stopped: a
    release within the hour unless the weapon is W01 (R07), a HARD checklist item failed or unanswered, a
{
 "R01": "2026-12-31T00:00:00+00:00"
}

def validate(raw: Any, expected: AssessmentInput) -> AssessmentV1:
    """Parse and check an answer: the schema, then the bar it judged. Raises ``ValueError``."""
    try:
        answer = raw if isinstance(raw, AssessmentV1) else AssessmentV1.model_validate(raw)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(
            f"the answer does not match schema {SCHEMA_VERSION}: {exc.error_count()} errors"
        ) from exc
    if answer.bar_close_utc != expected.bar_close_utc:
        raise ValueError(f"the answer judged bar {answer.bar_close_utc}, not {expected.bar_close_utc}")
    reasons = [r.strip()[:MAX_REASON_CHARS] for r in answer.reasons if r.strip()]
    return answer.model_copy(update={"reasons": reasons})

    def on_bar(self, now: pd.Timestamp, closes: list[float]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """One cycle at ``now`` (the last M1 bar's close); ``closes`` = the M1 closes since the last cycle.
        Returns the AI's decision record when it was woken, else None. Never raises."""
        try:
            return self._cycle(now, closes)
        except Exception as exc:  # a failed cycle must not stop the watcher; it is told, counted and written
            self.failures += 1
            log.exception("AI watcher cycle at %s failed (%d so far)", now, self.failures)
            self.store.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with (self.store / "watch_alerts.log").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                fh.write(f"{now.isoformat()} FAILED ({self.failures}): {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\n")
            return None

    def _cycle(self, now: pd.Timestamp, closes: list[float]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        self.store.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        kind = plan_due(now, self.state.last_plan)
        retry_at = self._plan_retry_at
        if kind is not None and retry_at is not None and now < retry_at:
            kind = None  # a failed plan waits PLAN_RETRY minutes (07-06: the quota stopped the calls)
        if kind is not None and self.plan is not None:  # the day's plan first (owner 2026-10-25, P26)
            rec = self.plan(now, kind)
            retry = (rec or {}).get("status") not in (None, "OK") and "plan" not in (rec or {})
            if not retry:
                self.state.last_plan = plan_key(now, kind)
            self._plan_retry_at = now + pd.Timedelta(minutes=PLAN_RETRY) if retry else None
            scenarios = ((rec or {}).get("plan") or {}).get("scenarios") or []
            self.state.plan_levels = [float(s["entry"]) for s in scenarios if s.get("entry") is not None]
            self.state.plan_orders = [plan_order(s) for s in scenarios]
            self.state.save(self.store / "watch_state.json")
        if now.minute % 5 == 0:
            self.state.behaviours = sorted(self._behaviours(now))
        last_call = pd.Timestamp(self.state.last_call) if self.state.last_call else None
        new = set(self.state.behaviours) - set(self.state.behaviours_at_call)
        reasons = wake_reasons(
            now,
            last_call,
            self.state.watch_above,
            self.state.watch_below,
            closes,
            new,
            kn.session(now) != "none",
            tuple(self.state.plan_levels),
            self.state.last_close,
            self.holding(),
        )
        if closes:
            self.state.last_close = closes[-1]
        if not reasons:
            self.state.save(self.store / "watch_state.json")
            return None
        spent = self._spent(now)
        if spent >= self.cap_usd:
            log.info("AI not woken at %s (%s): today's spend %.3f USD reached the cap", now, reasons, spent)
            return None
        session = self.store / f"session_{now.tz_convert(TH):%Y-%m-%d}.md"
        text = (
            tail(self.journal(), 3500)
            + "\n\nTHE AI'S OWN EARLIER CALLS:\n"
            + (tail(session.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), 1500) if session.exists() else "(none)")
            + orders_block(self.state.plan_orders, reasons)
            + position_tail(self.journal())
        )
        quiet = all(r.endswith("-minute check") for r in reasons)  # the regular check only: think less
        rec = self.decide(now, text, self.model, QUIET_EFFORT if quiet else EFFORT)
        d = rec.get("decision") or {}
        self.state = WatchState(
            now.isoformat(), d.get("watch_above"), d.get("watch_below"), list(self.state.behaviours),
            list(self.state.behaviours), self.state.last_plan, list(self.state.plan_levels),
            list(self.state.plan_orders), self.state.last_close,
        )  # fmt: skip
        self.state.save(self.store / "watch_state.json")
        with session.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(session_entry(now, reasons, rec))
        return rec
"""The AI trader lane in the engine (PLAN Â§A37, TAA-1805): on each closed M1 bar of one symbol the engine
hands the bars to the AI watcher (app/ai/watcher.py), which wakes the AI trader (app/ai/trader.py) when
something happens - the very modules the research's watcher and replays run.

The bars are fetched on the engine loop (the MT5 client is single-threaded); the watcher's cycle, which may
wait 5-15 s for the AI, runs on one background thread, so the loop never waits for it. A new bar while a call
is still running is not lost: its closes are carried to the next cycle. In TAA-1805 the decisions are recorded
only; TAA-1806 turns them into signals through the decision engine.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from concurrent.futures import Future, ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Protocol

import pandas as pd

from app.ai import plan as day_plan
from app.ai import trader
from app.ai.providers import AIProvider
from app.ai.watcher import AIWatcher
from app.config import AITraderConfig
from app.core.enums import Side, Timeframe
from app.market_data.data_models import CandleFrame
from app.news.releases import Release
from app.strategy import knowledge as kn
from app.strategy.ai_lane import AIOrder, AITrader

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# closed bars handed to the readers per frame: M5 from 1,400 x 5 M1 bars (rhythm), 2,600 bars of the rest, the
# D1 history for the zone grid's anchors (research/2026-10-23-entry: rhythm_scan, picture_read, knowledge)
TH_ZONE = "Asia/Bangkok"
COUNTS = {Timeframe.M1: 7200, Timeframe.M15: 2700, Timeframe.H1: 2700, Timeframe.H4: 2700, Timeframe.D1: 2500}


class ClosedCandles(Protocol):
    def closed_candles(self, symbol: str, tf: Timeframe, count: int) -> CandleFrame: ...


def position_note(
    positions: list[tuple[Side, float, float | None, float | None, datetime]], pending: AIOrder | None
) -> str:
    """What the system holds now, for the AI (2026-07-07 replay: it answered HOLD for an order that never
    filled and EXIT with nothing open): the open positions (side, entry, stop, target, opened) and the order
    waiting for its price."""
    lines = ["THE SYSTEM'S POSITION NOW (from the broker; HOLD / EXIT only for these):"]
    for side, entry, sl, tp, opened in positions:
        lines.append(
            f"- open {side.value} from {entry:.2f}, stop {sl}, target {tp}, opened "
            f"{pd.Timestamp(opened).tz_convert(TH_ZONE):%H:%M} Thai"
        )
    if pending is not None:
        lines.append(
            f"- waiting order {pending.side.value} at {pending.entry:.2f}, stop {pending.stop}, target "
            f"{pending.target}, until {pd.Timestamp(pending.valid_until).tz_convert(TH_ZONE):%H:%M} Thai"
        )
    if len(lines) == 1:
        lines.append("- none: no open position and no waiting order")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def to_frame(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Candle rows (``CANDLE_COLUMNS``) as the readers take them: indexed by the bar's open (UTC), the last
    copy of a bar kept - the same shape as the research's stored history (research zone_wait.load)."""
    out = df.assign(t=pd.to_datetime(df["open_time"], utc=True)).drop_duplicates("t", keep="last")
    return out.set_index("t").sort_index()[["open", "high", "low", "close", "tick_volume"]]


class AITraderLane:
    def __init__(
        self,
        cfg: AITraderConfig,
        candles: ClosedCandles,
        provider: AIProvider,
        releases: tuple[Release, ...],
        store: Path,
        journal: Path,
        *,
        background: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.candles = candles
        self.provider = provider
        self.releases = releases
        self.store = store
        self.journal_path = journal
        self._frames: kn.Frames | None = None
        self._last_open: pd.Timestamp | None = None
        self._checked: pd.Timestamp | None = None
        self._executor = (
            ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="ai-trader") if background else None
        )
        self._running: Future[dict[str, Any] | None] | None = None
        self.cycles = 0
        self._decision: dict[str, Any] | None = None  # the last finished decision, not yet handed over
        self._plan_orders: list[AIOrder] | None = None  # the last plan's orders, not yet handed over
        self.position = position_note([], None)  # set by the engine before each tick
        self.watcher = AIWatcher(
            store,
            frames=self._current_frames,
            decide=self._decide,
            journal=self._journal,
            cap_usd=cfg.daily_cap_usd,
            model=cfg.model,
            plan=self._plan,
            holding=lambda: "- none:" not in self.position,
        )

    # --- the engine loop ---

    def tick(self) -> bool:
        """Called on every engine cycle; starts at most one watcher cycle per new closed M1 bar, never waits.
        Returns True when one was started."""
        if self._running is not None:
            if not self._running.done():
                return False
            self._decision = self._running.result() or self._decision  # the watcher never raises
            self._running = None
        m1 = self.candles.closed_candles(self.cfg.symbol, Timeframe.M1, 2)
        if m1.last_open_time is None:
            return False
        opened = pd.Timestamp(m1.last_open_time)
        opened = opened.tz_localize("UTC") if opened.tzinfo is None else opened.tz_convert("UTC")
        if self._last_open is not None and opened <= self._last_open:
            return False
        self._frames = self.frames()
        now = opened + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1)  # the bar's close: everything read is closed by it
        since = self._checked if self._checked is not None else opened
        closes = [float(x) for x in self._frames.m1[self._frames.m1.index >= since].close]
        self._last_open, self._checked = opened, now
        self.cycles += 1
        if self._executor is None:
            self._decision = self.watcher.on_bar(now, closes) or self._decision
        else:
            self._running = self._executor.submit(self.watcher.on_bar, now, closes)
        return True

    @property
    def m1(self) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
        """The closed M1 bars of the last watcher cycle (None before the first)."""
        return None if self._frames is None else self._frames.m1

    def take_decision(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        """The AI's last finished decision, once (on the engine loop)."""
        rec, self._decision = self._decision, None
        return rec

    def take_plan_orders(self) -> list[AIOrder] | None:
        """The last day plan's orders, once (on the engine loop); None: no new plan."""
        orders, self._plan_orders = self._plan_orders, None
        return orders

    def frames(self) -> kn.Frames:
        """The closed bars of every frame the readers need, from the engine's candle service."""
        get = {
            tf: to_frame(self.candles.closed_candles(self.cfg.symbol, tf, n).df) for tf, n in COUNTS.items()
        }
        return kn.Frames(
            get[Timeframe.M1], get[Timeframe.M15], get[Timeframe.H1], get[Timeframe.H4], get[Timeframe.D1],
            self.releases,
        )  # fmt: skip

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._executor is not None:
            self._executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)

    # --- the watcher's callables (the background thread) ---

    def _current_frames(self) -> kn.Frames:
        if self._frames is None:
            raise RuntimeError("no bars fetched yet")
        return self._frames

    def _journal(self) -> str:
        text = self.journal_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if self.journal_path.exists() else ""
        return f"{text}\n\n{self.position}"  # at the end: the journal's tail keeps it

    def _plan(self, as_of: pd.Timestamp, kind: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        rec = day_plan.make_plan(
            self.provider, kn.load(), self._current_frames(), as_of, kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            model=self.cfg.model, store=self.store, journal=self.journal_path,
        )  # fmt: skip
        self._plan_orders = day_plan.plan_orders(rec)  # placed in advance (owner 2026-10-25)
        return rec

    def _suspensions(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        path = self.store / "suspended.json"
        rows: dict[str, str] = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.exists() else {}
        return rows

    def _decide(
        self, as_of: pd.Timestamp, text: str, model: str | None, effort: str = trader.EFFORT
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return trader.decide(
            self.provider, kn.load(), self._current_frames(), as_of, text,
            model=model, suspensions=self._suspensions(), store=self.store, source="watcher", effort=effort,
        )  # fmt: skip


def order_from(rec: dict[str, Any], pending_minutes: int) -> AIOrder | None:
    """An AI decision record as an order for the ``ai_trader`` strategy: a SELL / BUY with a size the brakes
    left ('half' / 'full') and its entry and stop; else None."""
    d = rec.get("decision") or {}
    size = rec.get("final_size")
    side = d.get("decision") if d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY") else rec.get("order_side")
    if side not in ("SELL", "BUY") or size not in ("half", "full"):
        return None
    if d.get("entry") is None or d.get("stop") is None:
        return None
    at = pd.Timestamp(rec["as_of"]).to_pydatetime()
    return AIOrder(
        side=Side.SELL if side == "SELL" else Side.BUY,
        entry=float(d["entry"]),
        stop=float(d["stop"]),
        target=None if d.get("target") is None else float(d["target"]),
        risk_scale=0.5 if size == "half" else 1.0,
        setup=str(d.get("setup", "")),
        reasons=tuple(str(x) for x in d.get("reasons", [])),
        kb_ids=tuple(str(x) for x in d.get("kb_ids", [])),
        confidence=int(d.get("confidence") or 0),
        decided_at=at,
        valid_until=at + timedelta(minutes=max(1, min(240, int(d.get("valid_minutes") or pending_minutes)))),
        key=f"step-{pd.Timestamp(rec['as_of']):%Y%m%d%H%M}",
        watch_above=None if d.get("watch_above") is None else float(d["watch_above"]),
        watch_below=None if d.get("watch_below") is None else float(d["watch_below"]),
        exit_level=None if d.get("exit_level") is None else float(d["exit_level"]),
        direct=d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY"),
    )


def hand_over(strategy: AITrader, symbol: str, rec: dict[str, Any], pending_minutes: int) -> str:
    """What the strategy does with the AI's decision: a new order (it replaces a waiting one), an exit of the
    lane's open position, or a WAIT that withdraws a waiting order. Returns what was done."""
    d = rec.get("decision") or {}
    if rec.get("map_stops_now"):  # the plan's orders against the map's stops now (C4 at the fill)
        strategy.recheck_plan_stops(symbol, rec["map_stops_now"])
    for side, vetoes in (rec.get("side_vetoes") or {}).items():  # a rule in force stops a side now (R01 ...)
        if vetoes:
            strategy.drop_plan_side(symbol, Side.SELL if side == "SELL" else Side.BUY)
    order = order_from(rec, pending_minutes)
    if order is not None:
        strategy.accept(symbol, order)
        return "order"
    if d.get("decision") == "EXIT":
        strategy.request_exit(symbol)
        return "exit"
    if d.get("decision") in ("SELL", "BUY"):  # an entry the brakes vetoed withdraws the order it replaced
        strategy.cancel(symbol)
        return "cancel"
    return "none"  # a WAIT keeps a waiting order (07-06: the 07:05 WAIT withdrew the 07:00 sell at 4,197.50)

# Find what the AI lane swallows (2026-10-27)

Read-only. Two other sessions are editing this repo; change nothing. Write only
`research/2026-10-24-replay/SWALLOWED.md`. Use `.venv\Scripts\python.exe`.

## The thread to pull

`app/ai/trader.suspended()` turns the owner's rule suspensions into the set of rules switched off. Given
a value that is not a timestamp it raises - it always did. On 2026-10-27 a prose note was written into
`data/replay/ai/store/suspended.json` and a whole replay day ran **to completion, with no warning, no log
line, and a normal-looking journal**, while every rule stayed in force. The experiment it was meant to
run therefore measured nothing, and the two runs it was compared against were identical - which is the
only reason it was noticed.

So an exception was raised and something ate it. **Find what.**

Then widen the question: **what else does the AI lane hide?** Walk every `except` in `app/ai/`,
`app/engine/ai_trader.py` and `app/strategy/ai_lane.py`, and for each one answer:
- what can reach it;
- what the system does afterwards - does it log, record, alert, or continue silently;
- and whether a reader of the resulting journal could tell that anything went wrong.

The replay machinery is how this system learns, so a failure it cannot show is a failure that teaches
the wrong lesson. Two already happened today: this one, and a watcher state left 17 months in the future
that made "AI woken 0 times" look like a decision instead of a bug.

## What to deliver

1. **The swallower**, named with file and line, and the proof (a reproduction is best).
2. **A table of every `except` in those modules**: what it catches, what it hides, and whether the
   journal would show it. Mark each SAFE (reported one way or another) or SILENT.
3. For every SILENT one: what a reader would wrongly conclude from a journal produced while it fired.
4. **The one change** that would have made today's failure visible, written as a concrete suggestion -
   do not implement it.

Say plainly where you found nothing. Do not propose a broad refactor; the system is running live on a
demo account tonight and the owner has had enough change for one day.

"""The watcher - the code watches every closed M1 bar, the AI is woken only when something happens (PLAN
Â§A37, TAA-1805; owner 2026-10-23: "is it too slow - should we send every TF?" and the budget "10-15 baht a
day"). Moved from research/2026-10-23-entry/watcher.py, which now drives it from the stored history; the
engine drives it from its own bars.

The AI is woken when:

- an M1 bar closed beyond a price the AI asked to watch (its last answer's ``watch_above`` / ``watch_below``);
- a new behaviour of the moving kind appeared (sweep, break, climax, runaway, handover) that was not there at
  its last call - at most once per ``COOLDOWN`` minutes;
- ``HEARTBEAT`` minutes passed since its last call, inside the owner's windows.

A Thai day's spend by the watcher's own calls is capped (``DAILY_CAP_USD``). A failed cycle never stops the
watcher: the error is logged, counted and written to ``watch_alerts.log`` (2026-10-23 23:45: the research
watcher died silently for 20 minutes when a file moved).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pandas as pd

from app.ai.plan import plan_due, plan_key
from app.ai.trader import EFFORT, QUIET_EFFORT
from app.strategy import knowledge as kn

log = logging.getLogger(__name__)

TH = "Asia/Bangkok"
HEARTBEAT, COOLDOWN = 30, 15  # minutes (the 30-minute check also keeps the 1-hour prompt cache warm)
MOVING = {"sweep", "break", "climax", "runaway", "handover"}
MODEL = "claude-sonnet-5-5"  # the owner's budget - about 0.01 USD a call with the cache
DAILY_CAP_USD = 0.40  # about 14 baht: no call once a Thai day's watcher spend reaches it
SOURCE = "watcher"


def wake_reasons(
    now: pd.Timestamp,
    last_call: pd.Timestamp | None,
    above: float | None,
    below: float | None,
    closes: list[float],
    new_behaviours: set[str],
    in_window: bool,
    plan_levels: tuple[float, ...] = (),
    prev_close: float | None = None,
    holding: bool = False,
) -> list[str]:
    """Why the AI should be woken now (empty = let it sleep). ``closes`` = the M1 closes since the last check,
    ``new_behaviours`` = the behaviours present now and not at the AI's last call, ``in_window`` = inside the
    owner's windows (the regular check runs only there), ``plan_levels`` = the entries of the day plan's
    scenarios: a close arriving within PLAN_NEAR of one wakes it (``prev_close`` = the close before
    ``closes``; owner 2026-10-25: be awake at the plan's level to trade it on the spot). Outside the windows
    it sleeps unless ``holding`` (an open position or a waiting order to manage): R09 stops new trades there,
    and 34 of the 66 calls of 2026-07-06 were spent there (owner 2026-10-25: "60-odd alerts - something is
    wrong"); a watched price wakes it again only WATCH_COOLDOWN minutes after its last call."""
    if last_call is None:
        return ["the first call"]
    if not in_window and not holding:
        return []
    out = []
    minutes = (now - last_call).total_seconds() / 60
    if minutes >= WATCH_COOLDOWN:
        if above is not None and any(c > above for c in closes):
            out.append(f"an M1 close over {above:.2f}")
        if below is not None and any(c < below for c in closes):
            out.append(f"an M1 close under {below:.2f}")
    moving = sorted(new_behaviours & MOVING)
    if moving and minutes >= COOLDOWN:
        out.append("new: " + ", ".join(moving))
    if in_window and minutes >= HEARTBEAT:
        out.append(f"{HEARTBEAT}-minute check")
    path = ([prev_close] if prev_close is not None else []) + list(closes)
    for lv in plan_levels if minutes >= WATCH_COOLDOWN else ():  # 07-06 07:09-07:11: woken every minute
        near = [abs(c - lv) <= PLAN_NEAR for c in path]
        if any(near[1:] if prev_close is not None else near) and not (prev_close is not None and near[0]):
            out.append(f"at the plan's level {lv:.2f}")
    return out


def position_tail(journal: str) -> str:
    """The position note at the very end of the text, so no cut can drop it (07-06, ISSUES 11)."""
    k = journal.rfind("THE SYSTEM'S POSITION NOW")
    return "" if k < 0 else "\n\n" + journal[k:]


def plan_order(s: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """A day plan's scenario as the order it stands for."""
    return (
        f"{s.get('name')} {s.get('side')} ({s.get('where')}): entry {s.get('entry')}, stop {s.get('stop')}, "
        f"take-profit {s.get('take_profit')} - trigger: {s.get('trigger')}"
    )


def orders_block(orders: list[str], reasons: list[str]) -> str:
    """The plan's orders as the default answer (owner 2026-10-25, 06-07 Jul: every planned trade printed and
    the AI waited for more each time)."""
    if not orders:
        return ""
    at = [r for r in reasons if r.startswith("at the plan's level")]
    head = (
        "\n\nTHE DAY PLAN'S ORDERS - THE DEFAULT ANSWER (L20): when price is at or through a scenario's "
        "trigger, that scenario's order IS your decision - at market, or pending at its entry - unless a "
        "HARD "
    user = content(
        as_of, tail(journal, JOURNAL_CHARS), f, brief, reads, last_close(fr.m1, as_of), levels, liq
    )
    return system_prompt(entries), user, f


def decide(
    provider: AIProvider,
    entries: list[dict[str, Any]],
    fr: kn.Frames,
    as_of: pd.Timestamp,
    journal: str,
    *,
    model: str | None = None,
    suspensions: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    store: Path | None = None,
    source: str = "manual",
    effort: str = EFFORT,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """One AI decision at ``as_of`` with the code's brakes. ``store``: a folder for the raw prompts and
    answers (raw/<Thai time>_1_system.txt, _2_user.txt, _3_answer.json) and decisions.jsonl."""
    off = suspended(suspensions or {}, as_of)
    system, user, f = prompts(entries, fr, as_of, journal, off)
    stem = f"{as_of.tz_convert(TH):%Y-%m-%d_%H%M}"
    if store is not None:
        raw = store / "raw"
        raw.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (raw / f"{stem}_1_system.txt").write_text(system, encoding="utf-8")  # the owner sees what was asked
        (raw / f"{stem}_2_user.txt").write_text(user, encoding="utf-8")
    ans = provider.ask(
        f"step-{as_of:%Y%m%d%H%M}", system, user, StepDecision, model=model, effort=effort, cache_system=True
    )
    rec: dict[str, Any] = {
        "as_of": as_of.isoformat(),
        "source": source,
        "status": ans.status.value,
        "model": ans.model,
        "cost_usd": ans.cost_usd,
        "tokens": {
            "input": ans.input_tokens,
            "cache_write": ans.cache_write_tokens,
            "cache_read": ans.cache_read_tokens,
            "output": ans.output_tokens,
        },
        "latency_ms": ans.latency_ms,
        "detail": ans.detail,
    }
    if ans.parsed is not None:
        d: StepDecision = ans.parsed
        braked = d  # a WAIT with its pending order is braked as that entry (07-07, ISSUES 26)
        if d.decision == "WAIT" and d.wait_order != "none" and d.entry is not None and d.stop is not None:
            braked = d.model_copy(
                update={"decision": d.wait_order, "size": "half" if d.size == "none" else d.size}
            )
            rec.update(order_side=d.wait_order)
        size, vetoes = brakes(entries, f, braked, frozenset(off))
        check = stop_check(braked, f)
        dumped = d.model_dump()
        if braked.decision in ("BUY", "SELL") and check is not None and not check["beyond"]:
            if c4_keeps_r(braked, check["code"]):  # C4 by the code: the stop goes out past the zone
                dumped["stop"] = check["code"]
                rec.update(stop_widened={"from": d.stop, "to": check["code"]})
            else:  # 07-08 07:25: the AI's stop sat 42 short of the zone and was run
                size, vetoes = "none", [*vetoes, "C4 stop inside the liquidate zone"]
        held = [k for k in off if any(e["id"] == k and kn.applies(e, f) for e in entries)]
        rec.update(decision=dumped, final_size=size, vetoed_by=vetoes, suspended_applying=held)
        # the shadows with a stand-aside action: the trades they would have stopped (recorded, never applied)
        shadows = [dict(e, kind="rule") for e in entries if e["kind"] == "shadow" and e.get("action")]
        rec.update(shadow_vetoes=brakes(shadows, f, d)[1])
        rec.update(stop_check=check)
        rec.update(map_stops_now={"SELL": f.get("sell_stop_beyond"), "BUY": f.get("buy_stop_beyond")})
        code = weapons_in_play(entries, f)  # both sides of the picture: the code's reading and the AI's
        rec.update(weapons_in_play=code, same_picture=same_picture(code, d.weapon))
        rec.update(side_vetoes={s: rule_vetoes(entries, f, s, frozenset(off)) for s in ("BUY", "SELL")})
        if d.decision in ("BUY", "SELL"):  # the decision checklist, answered before the trade
            rec.update(checklist=checklist_gaps(d))
    if store is not None:
        (store / "raw" / f"{stem}_3_answer.json").write_text(
            json.dumps(rec, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False), "utf-8"
        )
        with (store / "decisions.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            fh.write(json.dumps(rec) + "\n")
    return rec

"""The AI trader's replay journal, one Thai day at a time (TAA-1807; owner 2026-10-24: "go day by day", a weekly
summary as the subtotal; accounts 100 USD at 7.5 % and 300 USD at 3 %, at least 0.01 lot).

One day = 07:00 Thai to 07:00 Thai the next day (00:00-24:00 UTC). The bot's own backtest runs it with the AI lane
(app.cli backtest --lane ai --ticks --ai-answers ...): the live watcher, AI trader, brakes, decision engine and tick
fills; the AI's answers come from the replay store (by input hash) or, when not stored yet, from a fresh Sonnet
session through the Claude Code CLI (no tools: it sees only the prompt). The watcher's files (decisions, prompts,
session notes, watch state) and the AI's journal.md carry over from day to day in STORE.

The 300 USD account is the engine's own run (3 % a trade, the AI's 'half' halves it). The 100 USD account takes
the same trades sized at 7.5 % of its equity (min 0.01 lot, 1 lot = 100 oz), as the PLAN-1 journal did - the AI's
prompt carries neither the account nor the positions, so the decisions are the same for both.

Labels on every report: the knowledge base was partly measured on these months (owner 2026-10-24: use it all,
labelled); the model's training data runs to about June 2026; the answer arrives at the bar close here, 5-60 s
later live; an open trade is closed at the day's end (07:00 Thai).

    .venv/Scripts/python research/2026-10-24-replay/day.py 2026-07-06          # replay and report the day

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import math
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parents[1]
# the answerer (owner 2026-10-26: "when running tests, work with Codex - the AI now on Sonnet to use
# GPT-6-Luna"): the newest Codex CLI of the VS Code ChatGPT extension; the Claude CLI if none is installed

    if _CODEX

        r"C:\Users\korap\.vscode\extensions\anthropic.claude-code-2.1.306-win32-x64\resources\native-binary\claude.exe"
    )

MODEL = "gpt-6-luna" if _CODEX else "claude-sonnet-5-5"

START = {"acct300": 300.0, "acct100": 100.0}
RISK100 = (
    0.18  # owner 2026-10-25: covers the median stop past the liquidation zone at 0.01 lot (17.7 USD in July)
)
TH = "Asia/Bangkok"


def balances(day: date) -> dict[str, float]:
    """The two accounts at the start of ``day``: the last ledger row before it."""
    out = dict(START)
    if LEDGER.exists():
        for row in csv.DictReader(LEDGER.open(encoding="utf-8")):
            if row["date"] < day.isoformat():
                out = {"acct300": float(row["acct300"]), "acct100": float(row["acct100"])}
    return out


def check_watcher_is_not_ahead(day: date) -> None:
    """Refuse a day that sits BEFORE the watcher's last call, instead of failing silently.

    The watcher's state carries from day to day, and every wake condition is ``minutes >= cooldown``
    counted from ``last_call``. Replaying an earlier date makes that difference negative, so NOTHING
    wakes the AI and the journal reads "0 trades" as if it had looked and declined. 2025-02-25 was
    replayed against a state left at 2026-07-09 and produced exactly that (2026-10-27).
    """
    state = RUN / "store/watch_state.json"
    if not state.exists():
        return
    last = json.loads(state.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("last_call")
    if last and pd.Timestamp(last).date() > day:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"the watcher's last call is {pd.Timestamp(last):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M}, AFTER {day}: every wake "
            "condition counts minutes from it and would go negative, so the AI would never be woken. "
            f"Replay forwards, or clear last_call in {state} first (keep a copy)."
        )


def replay(day: date, bal300: float) -> None:
    check_watcher_is_not_ahead(day)
    out = RUN / "days" / day.isoformat()
    cmd = [
        sys.executable, "-m", "app.cli", "backtest", "--lane", "ai", "--strategies", "ai_trader",
        "--server", "FBS-Demo", "--symbols", "XAUUSD", "--ticks", "data/ticks",
        "--start", day.isoformat(), "--end", (day + timedelta(days=1)).isoformat(),
        "--balance", f"{bal300:.2f}", "--out", str(out), "--preset", "aggressive", "--risk", "6",
        "--ai-answers", str(RUN / "answers"), "--ai-store", str(RUN / "store"), "--ai-cli", str(CLI),
        "--ai-model", MODEL,
    ]  # fmt: skip
    subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, check=True)  # noqa: S603


def size100(equity: float, risk_frac: float, stop_usd: float) -> float:
    """Lots for the 100 USD account: its risk over the stop's USD a lot, floored to 0.01, never below the 0.01
    minimum (P15: the account's size never vetoes a trade); 0 only for an empty account."""
    if equity <= 0 or stop_usd <= 0:
        return 0.0
    lots = math.floor(equity * risk_frac / (stop_usd * 100) / 0.01 + 1e-9) * 0.01
    return round(max(lots, 0.01), 2)  # P15 (owner): the 0.01-lot minimum is recorded, never a veto


KEYS = ("price", "session", "h1_vol", "h1_atr", "yesterday", "phase", "m15_rhythm", "m15_leg", "h1_rhythm",
        "h4_rhythm", "d1_rhythm", "h4_big", "d1_big", "line_dist_step", "line_break", "sweep", "climax", "runaway",
        "release_min")  # fmt: skip


def wake_reasons(at: pd.Timestamp) -> str:
    """Why the watcher woke the AI at ``at``: the heading of its entry in the Thai day's session notes."""
    th = at.tz_convert(TH)
    path = RUN / "store" / f"session_{th:%Y-%m-%d}.md"
    if path.exists():
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
            if line.startswith(f"### {th:%H:%M} Thai - the AI woken by: "):
                return line.split("woken by: ", 1)[1]
    return "?"


def readings(at: pd.Timestamp) -> dict:
    """The readings the AI was given (the stored prompt's READINGS block)."""
    path = RUN / "store" / "raw" / f"{at.tz_convert(TH):%Y-%m-%d_%H%M}_2_user.txt"
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    block = text.split("READINGS (knowledge base features):\n", 1)[1].split("\n\n", 1)[0]
    return json.loads(block)


def steps(decisions: list[dict]) -> None:
    """Every wake in full: why, what the AI read, what it decided and why, what it waits for, what it watches."""
    print("\n## Step by step\n")
    for r in decisions:
        at = pd.Timestamp(r["as_of"])
        d = r.get("decision") or {}
        f = readings(at)
        seen = ", ".join(f"{k} {f[k]}" for k in KEYS if k in f)
        print(f"### {at.tz_convert(TH):%H:%M} Thai - woken by: {wake_reasons(at)}")
        print(f"- read: {seen}")
        veto = f", vetoed by {', '.join(r['vetoed_by'])}" if r.get("vetoed_by") else ""
        print(
            f"- **{d.get('decision', r['status'])}** weapon {d.get('weapon', '-')} (size {r.get('final_size')}{veto}; confidence "
            f"{d.get('confidence')}): {d.get('setup', r.get('detail', ''))}"
        )
        if d.get("entry") is not None:
            print(f"- order: entry {d.get('entry')}, stop {d.get('stop')}, target {d.get('target')}")
            sc = r.get("stop_check")
            if sc:
                where = "past the liquidation zone" if sc["beyond"] else "SHORT of the liquidation zone"
                print(f"- stop check: the AI's {sc['ai']} vs the code's {sc['code']} - {where}")
        print(
            f"- the code sees: {', '.join(r.get('weapons_in_play') or []) or 'no weapon'}; the AI: {d.get('weapon')} "
            f"({'the same picture' if r.get('same_picture') else 'a different picture'})"

    name = "claude-cli"

    def __init__(
        self, exe: Path, clock: Clock, *, model: str = "claude-sonnet-5-5", effort: str = "medium"
    ) -> None:
        self.exe = exe
        self.clock = clock
        self.model = model
        self.effort = effort

    def assess(self, request: AssessmentInput) -> Assessment:
        return Assessment(Status.SKIPPED, request.signal_key, created_at=self.clock.now_utc(), detail="cli")

    def command(self, system_file: Path, schema: dict[str, Any], model: str, effort: str) -> list[str]:
        return [
            str(self.exe), "-p", "--model", model, "--effort", effort,
            "--tools", "", "--strict-mcp-config", "--setting-sources", "", "--no-session-persistence",
            "--exclude-dynamic-system-prompt-sections", "--system-prompt-file", str(system_file),
            "--output-format", "json", "--json-schema", json.dumps(schema),
        ]  # fmt: skip

    def ask(
        self,
        key: str,
        system: str,
        content: str,
        output_format: type[BaseModel],
        *,
        model: str | None = None,
        effort: str | None = None,
        cache_system: bool = False,
    ) -> Answer:
        used, level = model or self.model, effort or self.effort
        ans = self._once(system, content, output_format, used, level)
        if ans.status is Status.ERROR:
            log.warning("Claude CLI error (%s); one retry in %.0f s", ans.detail[:200], CLI_RETRY_SECONDS)
            time.sleep(CLI_RETRY_SECONDS)
            ans = self._once(system, content, output_format, used, level)
        return ans

    def _once(
        self, system: str, content: str, output_format: type[BaseModel], used: str, level: str
    ) -> Answer:
        now: datetime = self.clock.now_utc()
        started = time.perf_counter()
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="taa-ai-") as tmp:  # an empty folder: nothing to read
            system_file = Path(tmp) / "system.txt"
            system_file.write_text(system, encoding="utf-8")
            try:
                run = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603  # nosec B603 - a fixed executable, an argument list, no shell
                    self.command(system_file, output_format.model_json_schema(), used, level),
                    input=content, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", cwd=tmp,
                    timeout=CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, check=False,
                )  # fmt: skip
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                return Answer(Status.TIMEOUT, model=used, created_at=now, detail="the CLI timed out")
            except OSError as exc:
                return Answer(
                    Status.ERROR, model=used, created_at=now, detail=f"the CLI did not start: {exc}"
                )
        latency = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 1)
        return parse_cli(run.stdout, output_format, used, now, latency, run.stderr)


class CodexCLIProvider:
    """The Codex CLI (``codex exec``) as the answerer: one fresh session per call in an empty folder."""

    name = "codex-cli"

    def __init__(self, exe: Path, clock: Clock, *, model: str = "gpt-6-luna", effort: str = "medium") -> None:
        self.exe = exe
        self.clock = clock
        self.model = model
        self.effort = effort

    def assess(self, request: AssessmentInput) -> Assessment:
        return assess_with(self, request)  # the entry review too (AI_MODE veto / advisory)

    def command(self, folder: Path, schema_file: Path, out_file: Path, model: str, effort: str) -> list[str]:
        return [
            str(self.exe), "exec", "-m", model, "-c", f'model_reasoning_effort="{effort}"',
            "-s", "read-only", "--ephemeral", "--skip-git-repo-check", "-C", str(folder),
            "--output-schema", str(schema_file), "-o", str(out_file), "--json", "-",
        ]  # fmt: skip

    def ask(
        self,
        key: str,
        system: str,
        content: str,
        output_format: type[BaseModel],
        *,
        model: str | None = None,
        effort: str | None = None,
        cache_system: bool = False,
    ) -> Answer:
        used, level = self.model, effort or self.effort  # the caller's model names a Claude one: Codex's own
        ans = self._once(system, content, output_format, used, level)
        if ans.status is Status.ERROR:
            log.warning("Codex CLI error (%s); one retry in %.0f s", ans.detail[:200], CLI_RETRY_SECONDS)
            time.sleep(CLI_RETRY_SECONDS)
            ans = self._once(system, content, output_format, used, level)
        return ans

    def _once(
        self, system: str, content: str, output_format: type[BaseModel], used: str, level: str
    ) -> Answer:
        now: datetime = self.clock.now_utc()
        started = time.perf_counter()
        schema = output_format.model_json_schema()
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="taa-ai-") as tmp:
            folder = Path(tmp) / "empty"  # nothing to read
            folder.mkdir()
            schema_file, out_file = Path(tmp) / "schema.json", Path(tmp) / "answer.json"
            schema_file.write_text(json.dumps(strict_schema(schema)), encoding="utf-8")
            prompt = f"{CODEX_RULES}\n\n# SYSTEM\n{system}\n\n# INPUT\n{content}"
            try:
                run = subprocess.run(  # noqa: S603  # nosec B603 - a fixed executable, an argument list, no shell
                    self.command(folder, schema_file, out_file, used, level),
                    input=prompt, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", cwd=folder,
                    timeout=CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, check=False,
                )  # fmt: skip
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                return Answer(Status.TIMEOUT, model=used, created_at=now, detail="the CLI timed out")
            except OSError as exc:
                return Answer(
                    Status.ERROR, model=used, created_at=now, detail=f"the CLI did not start: {exc}"
                )
            answer = out_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if out_file.exists() else ""
        latency = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 1)
        return parse_codex(run.stdout, answer, schema, output_format, used, now, latency, run.stderr)


def find_codex() -> Path | None:
    """The newest Codex CLI of the VS Code ChatGPT extension (where the owner installed Codex), or None."""
    found = sorted(Path.home().glob(".vscode/extensions/openai.chatgpt-*/bin/windows-x86_64/codex.exe"))
    return found[-1] if found else None


CODEX_RULES = (
    "Answer from the text below only. Do not run any command, read any file or use any tool: the answer is "
    "refused if you do. Reply with the JSON the schema asks for; a field you leave empty is null."
)
TOOL_EVENTS = ("command", "exec", "file", "tool", "patch", "search", "mcp")  # item types that read or act


def strict_schema(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """``schema`` in OpenAI's strict form: every object closed (additionalProperties false) with every
    property required, a property that was optional made nullable, and the keyword strict mode refuses
def unstrict(value: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], defs: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    """``value`` without the nulls strict mode put in optional fields that do not allow null themselves."""
    if "$ref" in schema:
        schema = defs.get(schema["$ref"].split("/")[-1], {})
    if isinstance(value, dict) and "properties" in schema:
        props = schema["properties"]
        return {
            k: unstrict(v, props.get(k, {}), defs)
            for k, v in value.items()
            if not (v is None and k in props and not allows_null(props[k]))
        }
    if isinstance(value, list) and "items" in schema:
        return [unstrict(v, schema["items"], defs) for v in value]
    return value


def parse_codex(
    stdout: str,
    answer: str,
    schema: dict[str, Any],
    output_format: type[BaseModel],
    model: str,
    now: datetime,
    latency: float,
    stderr: str = "",
) -> Answer:
    """The Codex CLI's events (``--json``) and last message as an :class:`Answer`: refused when an event shows
    a command, file read or tool call; else the message validated against the schema. Codex runs on the
    owner's plan: no cost is counted."""
    usage: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for line in stdout.splitlines():
        try:
            ev = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        item = ev.get("item") or {}
        kind = str(item.get("type") or item.get("item_type") or "")
        if any(word in kind for word in TOOL_EVENTS):
            detail = f"the answerer used a tool ({kind}): refused"
            return Answer(Status.ERROR, model=model, created_at=now, latency_ms=latency, detail=detail)
        if ev.get("type") == "turn.completed":
            usage = ev.get("usage") or {}
        if ev.get("type") in ("turn.failed", "error"):
            detail = str((ev.get("error") or {}).get("message") or ev.get("message") or "")[:500]
            return Answer(Status.ERROR, model=model, created_at=now, latency_ms=latency, detail=detail)
    spend: dict[str, Any] = {
        "model": model,
        "input_tokens": int(usage.get("input_tokens", 0)),
        "output_tokens": int(usage.get("output_tokens", 0)),
        "cache_read_tokens": int(usage.get("cached_input_tokens", 0)),
        "latency_ms": latency,
        "created_at": now,
    }
    try:
        raw = json.loads(answer)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return Answer(Status.ERROR, detail=(answer or stderr.strip()[-300:] or "no answer")[:500], **spend)
    try:
        parsed = output_format.model_validate(unstrict(raw, schema, schema.get("$defs", {})))
    except ValidationError as exc:
        return Answer(Status.INVALID, detail=str(exc)[:500], **spend)
    return Answer(Status.OK, parsed=parsed, **spend)


def parse_cli(
    stdout: str, output_format: type[BaseModel], model: str, now: datetime, latency: float, stderr: str = ""
) -> Answer:
    """The CLI's JSON result as an :class:`Answer`: the structured output validated against the schema, the
    model's tokens and list-price cost."""
    try:
        out = json.loads(stdout)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return Answer(
            Status.ERROR, model=model, created_at=now, latency_ms=latency, detail=(stderr or stdout)[:500]
        )
    usage = (out.get("modelUsage") or {}).get(model) or {}
    spend: dict[str, Any] = {
        "model": model,
        "input_tokens": int(usage.get("inputTokens", 0)),
        "output_tokens": int(usage.get("outputTokens", 0)),
        "cache_write_tokens": int(usage.get("cacheCreationInputTokens", 0)),
        "cache_read_tokens": int(usage.get("cacheReadInputTokens", 0)),
        "cost_usd": round(float(usage.get("costUSD", 0.0)), 6),
        "latency_ms": latency,
        "created_at": now,
    }
    if out.get("is_error") or out.get("structured_output") is None:
        why = [str(out.get(k)) for k in ("result", "terminal_reason", "api_error_status") if out.get(k)]
        return Answer(
            Status.ERROR,
            detail="; ".join(why + [stderr.strip()[-200:]] * bool(stderr.strip()))[:500],
            **spend,
        )
    try:
        parsed = output_format.model_validate(out["structured_output"])
    except ValidationError as exc:
        return Answer(Status.INVALID, detail=str(exc)[:500], **spend)
    return Answer(Status.OK, parsed=parsed, **spend)


def spent_today(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], now: pd.Timestamp) -> float:
    """The watcher's AI spend (USD) on the Thai day of ``now`` (manual and test calls are not counted - owner
    2026-10-23: the building cost is outside the budget)."""
    day = now.tz_convert(TH).date()
    return sum(
        float(r.get("cost_usd") or 0)
        for r in rows
        if r.get("source") == SOURCE and pd.Timestamp(r["as_of"]).tz_convert(TH).date() == day
    )


def session_entry(now: pd.Timestamp, reasons: list[str], rec: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """The decision as the session's markdown (the AI reads its own earlier calls from it)."""
    d = rec.get("decision") or {}
    veto = ", vetoed by " + ", ".join(rec["vetoed_by"]) if rec.get("vetoed_by") else ""
    weapon = f" {d['weapon']}" if d.get("weapon") else ""
    return (
        f"\n### {now.tz_convert(TH):%H:%M} Thai - the AI woken by: {'; '.join(reasons)}\n"
        f"- **{d.get('decision', rec.get('status'))}**{weapon} (size {rec.get('final_size')}"
        f"{veto}; confidence {d.get('confidence')}): {d.get('setup', rec.get('detail', ''))}\n"
        f"- entry {d.get('entry')}, stop {d.get('stop')}, target {d.get('target')}; watch above "
        f"{d.get('watch_above')} / below {d.get('watch_below')}\n- trigger: {d.get('trigger')}\n"
        + "".join(f"- {r}\n" for r in d.get("reasons", []))
        + f"- cost {rec.get('cost_usd')} USD, {rec.get('latency_ms')} ms\n"
    )

@dataclass
class WatchState:
    last_call: str | None = None
    watch_above: float | None = None
    watch_below: float | None = None
    behaviours_at_call: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    behaviours: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    last_plan: str | None = None  # 'YYYY-MM-DD:kind' of the last day plan written (app/ai/plan.py)
    plan_levels: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)  # its scenarios' entries: wake there
    plan_orders: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # its scenarios as orders, shown at every call
    last_close: float | None = None  # the last M1 close seen (arriving at a plan level)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path) -> WatchState:
        if not path.exists():
            return cls()
        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        return cls(**{k: raw.get(k) for k in cls.__dataclass_fields__ if k in raw})

    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:
        path.write_text(json.dumps(self.__dict__), encoding="utf-8")

"""The engine's AI notes on advisory (TAA-1305, TAA-1304): one background call at a time, never blocking.

:meth:`AINotes.tick` runs in the engine cycle after the scanner. It collects a finished call, and when none
is running picks the next job, newest first:

1. an **opinion** on the newest OPEN opportunity without a note that is younger than
   ``ai.advisory.max_age_minutes`` and scores at least ``ai.advisory.min_score``;
2. a **ranking narrative** of the latest snapshot, at most every ``ranking_every_hours``;
3. an **analytics narrative** of the last ``analytics_days`` of closed PLAN shadow trades, at most every
   ``analytics_every_hours``.

The call itself runs on one worker thread (PLAN D6: "AI failures must never stop monitoring") and writes its
``ai_notes`` row from there. Every attempt is recorded, answered or not, so a subject is asked once. The
budget (``ai.advisory.max_calls_per_day`` / ``max_cost_per_day_usd`` per UTC day) is counted from
``ai_notes``; when it is used up no call is made and nothing is recorded.
"""

        self.stats = NotesStats()
        # one worker thread; without it (tests) the call runs inside tick()
        self._executor = (
            ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="ai-notes") if background else None
        )
        self._running: Future[None] | None = None

    @property
    def active(self) -> bool:
        return self.config.enabled and self.provider.name != "none"

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self._executor is not None:
            self._executor.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)

    # cycle -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def tick(self) -> bool:
        """Start the next call if none is running. Returns True when a call was started."""
        if not self.active:
            return False
        if self._running is not None:
            if not self._running.done():
                return False
            exc = self._running.exception()
            self._running = None
            if exc is not None:
                self._failed(exc)
        now = self.clock.now_utc()
        request = self.next_request(now)
        if request is None:
            return False
        calls, cost = self.spent_today(now)
        if calls >= self.config.max_calls_per_day or cost >= self.config.max_cost_per_day_usd:
            self.stats.budget_holds += 1
            return False
        if self._executor is None:
            try:
                self._call(request)
            except Exception as exc:  # advisory boundary, as on the worker thread
                self._failed(exc)
        else:
            self._running = self._executor.submit(self._call, request)
        return True

    def _failed(self, exc: BaseException) -> None:
        self.stats.failures += 1
        self.stats.last_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        log.error("AI note failed: %s", self.stats.last_error)

    def next_request(self, now: datetime) -> NoteRequest | None:
        with self.db.session() as sess:
            if self.config.opinions:
                since = now - timedelta(minutes=self.config.max_age_minutes)
                done = select(AINoteRow.subject).where(AINoteRow.kind == Kind.OPPORTUNITY.value)
                row = sess.scalar(
                    select(OpportunityRow)
                    .where(
                        OpportunityRow.created_at >= since,
                        OpportunityRow.status.in_(sorted(OPEN)),
                        OpportunityRow.score >= self.config.min_score,
                        OpportunityRow.opportunity_id.not_in(done),
                    )
                    .order_by(OpportunityRow.created_at.desc())
                    .limit(1)
                )
                if row is not None:
                    return opinion_request(row)
            if self.config.ranking_narrative and self._due(
                sess, Kind.RANKING, now, self.config.ranking_every_hours
            ):
                latest = sess.scalar(select(func.max(SuitabilitySnapshotRow.hour)))
                if latest is not None:
                    rows = list(
                        sess.scalars(
                            select(SuitabilitySnapshotRow).where(SuitabilitySnapshotRow.hour == latest)
                        )
                    )
                    request = ranking_request(rows)
                    if request is not None and sess.get(AINoteRow, (LOCAL_ENGINE, request.note_id)) is None:
                        return request
            if self.config.analytics_narrative and self._due(
                sess, Kind.ANALYTICS, now, self.config.analytics_every_hours
            ):
                since = now - timedelta(days=self.config.analytics_days)
                trades = list(
                    sess.scalars(
                        select(ShadowTradeRow).where(
                            ShadowTradeRow.variant == "PLAN",
                            ShadowTradeRow.source == "LIVE",
                            ShadowTradeRow.status == "CLOSED",
                            ShadowTradeRow.exit_at >= since,
                        )
                    )
                )
                request = analytics_request(trades, since, now)
                if request is not None and sess.get(AINoteRow, (LOCAL_ENGINE, request.note_id)) is None:
                    return request
        return None

    @staticmethod
    def _due(sess: Session, kind: Kind, now: datetime, every_hours: int) -> bool:
        last = sess.scalar(select(func.max(AINoteRow.created_at)).where(AINoteRow.kind == kind.value))
        return last is None or now - ensure_utc(last) >= timedelta(hours=every_hours)

    def spent_today(self, now: datetime) -> tuple[int, float]:
        start = datetime.combine(now.date(), time(0), tzinfo=now.tzinfo)
        with self.db.session() as sess:
            calls, cost = sess.execute(
                select(func.count(), func.coalesce(func.sum(AINoteRow.cost_usd), 0.0)).where(
                    AINoteRow.created_at >= start,
                    AINoteRow.created_at < start + timedelta(days=1),
                    AINoteRow.status.not_in(UNCOUNTED),
                )
            ).one()
        return int(calls), float(cost)

    # the call (worker thread) ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _call(self, request: NoteRequest) -> None:
        answer = self.provider.ask(request.note_id, request.prompt, request.to_json(), request.schema)
        status, detail = answer.status, answer.detail
        verdict = confidence = None
        reasons: tuple[str, ...] = ()
        text_en = text_th = ""
        if status is Status.OK:
            try:
                checked = check(answer.parsed, request)
            except ValueError as exc:
                status, detail = Status.INVALID, str(exc)[:500]
            else:
                verdict, confidence, reasons = checked.verdict, checked.confidence, checked.reasons
                text_en, text_th = checked.text_en, checked.text_th
        with self.db.session() as sess:
            if sess.get(AINoteRow, (LOCAL_ENGINE, request.note_id)) is None:
                sess.add(
                    AINoteRow(
                        note_id=request.note_id,
                        kind=request.kind.value,
                        subject=request.subject,
                        created_at=answer.created_at or self.clock.now_utc(),
                        status=status.value,
                        verdict=None if verdict is None else verdict.value,
                        confidence=confidence,
                        reasons=list(reasons),
                        text_en=text_en,
                        text_th=text_th,
                        facts=request.payload,
                        model=answer.model,
                        input_tokens=answer.input_tokens,
                        output_tokens=answer.output_tokens,
                        cost_usd=answer.cost_usd,
                        latency_ms=answer.latency_ms,
                        detail=detail,
                    )
                )
        self.stats.calls += 1
        self.stats.by_kind[request.kind.value] = self.stats.by_kind.get(request.kind.value, 0) + 1
        if status is Status.OK:
            self.stats.answered += 1
        log.info(
            "AI note %s: %s %s", request.note_id[:60], status.value, "" if verdict is None else verdict.value
"""The day's plan, written by the AI trader before it trades (owner 2026-10-25, KB P26, checklist E8: "before
trading each day, look at the market - yesterday's support / resistance and key levels, the likely trend, the
opportunities, the side with the edge, where price may turn or go on - and summarise it to me"; "you need the
levels on the smaller frames to trade during the day too").

At the start of each of the owner's windows (Asia 07:00 Thai = the morning plan; London and New York = an
update with the day's own M15 / M5 levels so far) the watcher asks the same AI, on the same readings, for a
:class:`DayPlan`. The plan goes into the AI's journal (so every later decision reads it), into ``plans.jsonl``
and the raw files of the store, and its readiness check is the forest items of the decision checklist answered
for the day plus the code's own facts. One code path for the replay and the engine (PLAN Â§A37).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.ai.providers import AIProvider
