Kind = Literal["morning", "london", "ny"]
KIND_OF_SESSION: dict[str, Kind] = {"asia": "morning", "london": "london", "ny": "ny"}
FOREST = tuple(k for k in ITEMS if k.startswith("A"))  # the day's readiness: the forest items A1 .. A8
READINGS = (
    "price", "d1_big", "d1_regime", "d1_rhythm", "h4_trend", "h4_rhythm", "h1_trend", "h1_rhythm",
    "m15_rhythm", "phase", "yesterday", "h1_vol", "h1_atr", "d1_atr20", "release_min", "runaway", "session",
    "asia_break",
)  # fmt: skip


class Level(BaseModel):
    price: float
    frame: Literal["D1", "H4", "H1", "M15", "M5"]
    role: Literal["support", "resistance", "both"]
    what: str = Field(description="what the level is, e.g. yesterday's high, M15 equal lows x3, round 4,200")


class Scenario(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(description="A, B, C ...")
    side: Literal["BUY", "SELL"]
    where: Literal["turn", "go on"] = Field(description="a turn at a level, or the move going on through it")
    entry: float | None = Field(
        description="the price where this trade is entered: a pending order sits there, and the AI is woken "
        "when price arrives"
    )
    trigger: str = Field(
        description="ONE price event at the entry - price reaching it (a pending order), or one M1 / M5 "
        "close back across the level (e.g. an M5 close back under 4,195) - never a chain of confirmations "
        "(L20)"
    )
    stop: float | None = Field(description="past the liquidate zone beyond the structure")
    take_profit: float | None = Field(
        description="the next swing, >= 5 USD, short of the next main line's band"
    )
    why: str


class DayPlan(BaseModel):
    condition: str = Field(
        description="the market now: the D1 side, the H4 / H1 trend, volatility, the phase"
    )
    big_levels: list[Level] = Field(
        description="support / resistance on D1 / H4 / H1 and yesterday's: its high / low, strong levels, "
        "zone lines with their bands, round numbers"
    )
    intraday_levels: list[Level] = Field(
        description="the levels to trade during the day on M15 / M5: the overnight / today's swings, equal "
        "highs / lows, the Asia range, volume nodes - the nearest ones above and below price"
    )
    trend_today: str = Field(description="the likely path today and why")
    opportunities: list[str] = Field(description="the trades the day may offer, in the owner's windows")
    edge_side: Literal["BUY", "SELL", "both", "none"] = Field(
        description="the side with the edge today (both: each at its own level)"
    )
    edge_why: str
    scenarios: list[Scenario] = Field(description="the points where price may turn or go on")
    releases: str = Field(description="today's scheduled releases and the windows")
    readiness: list[CheckAnswer] = Field(
        description="the forest items A1 .. A8 of the decision checklist answered for the day"
    )
    summary_th: str = Field(description="the plan in at most six short lines of Thai, for the owner")


TASK = {
    "morning": "THE MORNING PLAN (P26): before trading the day, write the day's plan (D1 names the side with "
    "the edge; H4 / H1 against it is a pullback whose end is the trade, L21)",
    "london": "THE LONDON UPDATE (P26): before the London window, update the day's plan",
    "ny": "THE NEW YORK UPDATE (P26): before the New York window, update the day's plan",
}


def plan_due(now: pd.Timestamp, last: str | None) -> Kind | None:
    """The plan due at ``now``: the window ``now`` is in, once a Thai day (``last`` = 'YYYY-MM-DD:kind')."""
    kind = KIND_OF_SESSION.get(kn.session(now))
    if kind is None:
        return None
    key = f"{now.tz_convert(TH):%Y-%m-%d}:{kind}"
    return None if key == last else kind


def plan_key(now: pd.Timestamp, kind: Kind) -> str:
    return f"{now.tz_convert(TH):%Y-%m-%d}:{kind}"


def plan_inputs(fr: kn.Frames, as_of: pd.Timestamp) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    """What the plan is written from (bars closed by ``as_of``): the readings, yesterday's and today's own
    levels, the readers' lines, the liquidity map (the stop side) and today's releases."""
    f = kn.features(fr, as_of)
    th = as_of.tz_convert(TH)
    start = pd.Timestamp(f"{th.date()} 07:00", tz=TH).tz_convert("UTC")
    # the previous trading day (Friday's on a Monday): its last M15 bar before today's 05:00 Thai
    before = fr.m15[fr.m15.index + pd.Timedelta(minutes=15) <= start - pd.Timedelta(hours=2)]
    prev_end = before.index[-1] + pd.Timedelta(minutes=15) if len(before) else start
    # day_levels' own D1 line there is the bar BEFORE that day: name the last closed D1 bar explicitly instead
    yesterday = [x for x in bh.day_levels(fr.m15, fr.d1, prev_end) if not x.startswith("yesterday's D1")]
    d1_done = fr.d1[fr.d1.index + pd.Timedelta(days=1) <= as_of]
    if len(d1_done):
        b = d1_done.iloc[-1]
        named = d1_done.index[-1] + pd.Timedelta(hours=12)  # the broker's day: its bar opens 21:00 UTC before
        yesterday.append(
            f"the last closed D1 bar ({named:%a %d %b}): high {b.high:.2f}, low {b.low:.2f}, "
            f"close {b.close:.2f}"
        )
    today = bh.day_levels(fr.m15, fr.d1, as_of) if as_of > start else []
    m5 = kn.m5_frame(fr.m1, as_of)
    liq = lq.liquidity_lines(fr.m1, m5, fr.m15, fr.h1, fr.d1, as_of)
    day_end = start + pd.Timedelta(days=1)
    rel = [x for x in fr.releases if start <= pd.Timestamp(x.at) < day_end]
    readings = {k: (round(v, 3) if isinstance(v, float) else v) for k, v in f.items() if k in READINGS}
    parts = [
        f"NOW: {th:%a %d %b %Y %H:%M} Thai time. Bars closed by this time only.",
        "READINGS:\n" + json.dumps(readings),
        "YESTERDAY'S OWN LEVELS (the previous Thai day):\n" + ("\n".join(yesterday) or "(none)"),
        "TODAY'S OWN LEVELS SO FAR (M15 swings, the day's high / low):\n"
        + ("\n".join(today) or "(none yet)"),
        "THE READERS' OUTPUT (rhythm per frame, pictures, the zone plan, strong levels):\n"
        + "\n".join(reader_lines(fr, as_of)),
        "THE LIQUIDITY MAP (liquidate zones - the stop side, never take-profit points):\n" + "\n".join(liq),
        "TODAY'S SCHEDULED US RELEASES (Thai time):\n"
        + (
            "\n".join(f"{pd.Timestamp(x.at).tz_convert(TH):%H:%M} {getattr(x, 'name', '')}" for x in rel)
            or "none"
        ),
        "THE OWNER'S WINDOWS: Asia 07:00-09:00, London 13:00-15:00 Thai, New York 08:00-11:30 New York time.",
    ]
    return "\n\n".join(parts), f


def readiness_facts(f: dict[str, Any], as_of: pd.Timestamp, fr: kn.Frames) -> list[str]:
    """The code's own readiness facts for the day: the bars' freshness, volatility (R01), releases (R07)."""
    closed = fr.m1.index[fr.m1.index + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1) <= as_of]
    last = closed[-1] + pd.Timedelta(minutes=1) if len(closed) else None
    fresh = last is not None and (as_of - last) <= pd.Timedelta(minutes=5)
    vol = f.get("h1_vol")
    return [
        f"bars current: {'yes' if fresh else 'NO'} (the last M1 close {last})",
        f"volatility (R01): h1_vol {vol:.2f} - {'stand aside' if vol > 1.1 else 'tradable'}"
        if isinstance(vol, float)
        else "volatility (R01): no reading",
        f"the nearest US release: {f.get('release_min')} minutes away (R07 within 60)",
    ]


def plan_markdown(plan: DayPlan, as_of: pd.Timestamp, kind: Kind, facts: list[str]) -> str:
    """The plan as the journal section the AI reads at every later step (and the owner reads)."""
    th = as_of.tz_convert(TH)
    title = {"morning": "the morning plan", "london": "the London update", "ny": "the New York update"}[kind]

    def lv(x: Level) -> str:
        return f"{x.price:.2f} {x.frame} {x.role} ({x.what})"

    lines = [
        f"## {th:%a %d %b %Y} - {title} ({th:%H:%M} Thai, written by the AI, P26)",
        f"- condition: {plan.condition}",
        "- big levels: " + "; ".join(lv(x) for x in plan.big_levels),
        "- intraday levels (M15 / M5): " + "; ".join(lv(x) for x in plan.intraday_levels),
        f"- the trend today: {plan.trend_today}",
        f"- the side with the edge: {plan.edge_side} - {plan.edge_why}",
    ]
    for s in plan.scenarios:
        lines.append(
            f"- {s.name} ({s.side}, {s.where}): {s.trigger}; stop {s.stop}, take-profit {s.take_profit} - "
            f"{s.why}"
        )
    lines.append("- opportunities: " + "; ".join(plan.opportunities))
    lines.append(f"- releases / windows: {plan.releases}")
    fails = [a.item for a in plan.readiness if a.verdict == "fail"]
    lines.append(f"- readiness (A1-A8): failed {', '.join(fails) or 'none'}; " + "; ".join(facts))
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def window_end(as_of: pd.Timestamp, kind: Kind) -> pd.Timestamp:
    """The end of the window a plan is written for: 09:00 / 15:00 Thai, 11:30 New York."""
    if kind == "ny":
        ny = as_of.tz_convert("America/New_York")
        return pd.Timestamp(f"{ny.date()} 11:30", tz="America/New_York").tz_convert("UTC")
    th = as_of.tz_convert(TH)
    return pd.Timestamp(f"{th.date()} {'09:00' if kind == 'morning' else '15:00'}", tz=TH).tz_convert("UTC")


def next_release(fr: kn.Frames, as_of: pd.Timestamp) -> str | None:
    """The next scheduled US release after ``as_of`` (ISO), None without one."""
    later = sorted(pd.Timestamp(x.at) for x in fr.releases if pd.Timestamp(x.at) > as_of)
    return later[0].isoformat() if later else None


def plan_orders(rec: dict[str, Any]) -> list[AIOrder]:
    """The plan's scenarios as pending orders, placed in advance and checked by the code (owner 2026-10-25:
    every planned trade of 06-07 Jul printed and none was taken): an entry, stop and take-profit point each;
    the stop moved out to the map's stop past the liquidate zone when nearer (C4); the take-profit point 5 USD
    or more from the entry (C5); no side a rule in force stops now (R01 / R07 / R09 ...); none against the D1
    regime (owner 2026-10-26: a trend needs the stop orders with it); half size."""
    plan = rec.get("plan")
    if plan is None:
        return []
    at = pd.Timestamp(rec["as_of"]).to_pydatetime()
    until = pd.Timestamp(rec["valid_until"]).to_pydatetime()
    if rec.get("next_release"):  # R07: an hour either side of a release is the news weapon's alone
        until = min(until, (pd.Timestamp(rec["next_release"]) - pd.Timedelta(minutes=60)).to_pydatetime())

 succeeded in 2060ms:
    def prompt(self) -> str:
        return {Kind.OPPORTUNITY: OPINION_PROMPT, Kind.RANKING: RANKING_PROMPT}.get(
            self.kind, ANALYTICS_PROMPT
        )

    @property
    def schema(self) -> type[BaseModel]:
        return OpinionV1 if self.kind is Kind.OPPORTUNITY else NarrativeV1


def _num(value: Any, digits: int = 6) -> float | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        f = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return round(f, digits) if math.isfinite(f) else None

def opinion_request(row: OpportunityRow) -> NoteRequest:
    """The numeric facts of one opportunity (no lot, money, equity or account)."""
class Checked:
    verdict: Verdict | None
    confidence: int | None
    reasons: tuple[str, ...]
    text_en: str
    text_th: str


def _clean(text: str, limit: int) -> str:
    return " ".join(text.split())[:limit]


def check(raw: Any, request: NoteRequest) -> Checked:
    """Parse and check an answer: the schema, the echoed subject and the claim filter.

    Raises ``ValueError``."""
    schema = request.schema
    try:
        answer = raw if isinstance(raw, schema) else schema.model_validate(raw)
    except ValidationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"the answer does not match {schema.__name__}: {exc.error_count()} errors") from exc
    if getattr(answer, "subject", None) != request.subject:
        raise ValueError(
            f"the answer describes {getattr(answer, 'subject', None)!r}, not {request.subject!r}"
        )
    if isinstance(answer, OpinionV1):
        reasons = tuple(_clean(r, MAX_REASON_CHARS) for r in answer.reasons if r.strip())
        result = Checked(
            answer.verdict,
            answer.confidence,
            reasons,
            _clean(answer.narrative_en, MAX_NARRATIVE_CHARS),
            _clean(answer.narrative_th, MAX_NARRATIVE_CHARS),
        )
    elif isinstance(answer, NarrativeV1):
        result = Checked(
            None,
            None,
            (),
            _clean(answer.text_en, MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS),
            _clean(answer.text_th, MAX_SUMMARY_CHARS),
        )
    else:  # pragma: no cover - request.schema is one of the two
        raise ValueError(f"unexpected answer type {type(answer).__name__}")
    for text in (result.text_en, result.text_th, *result.reasons):
        if CLAIMS.search(text):
            raise ValueError("the answer claims profit or certainty")
    if not result.text_en or not result.text_th:
        raise ValueError("the answer has an empty narrative")
    return result
        character from the knowledge base): a read costs a tenth of the input rate, the first write twice."""
        started = time.perf_counter()
        now = self.clock.now_utc()

        def done(status: Status, detail: str = "", **kw: Any) -> Answer:
            latency = round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000, 1)
            result = Answer(status, latency_ms=latency, created_at=now, detail=detail[:500], **kw)
            log.info(
                "AI %s %s %s: %s tokens=%s/%s cost=$%.4f %sms %s",
                output_format.__name__,
                key[:24],
                result.model or self.model,
                status.value,
                result.input_tokens,
                result.output_tokens,
                result.cost_usd,
                latency,
                detail[:120],
            )
            return result

        try:
            import anthropic
        except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - the engine requirements pin it
            return done(Status.ERROR, "the anthropic package is not installed")
        try:
            system_arg: Any = system
            if cache_system:
                system_arg = [
                    {"type": "text", "text": system, "cache_control": {"type": "ephemeral", "ttl": "1h"}}
                ]
            response = self.client.beta.messages.parse(
                model=model or self.model,
                max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS,
                system=system_arg,
                messages=[{"role": "user", "content": content}],
                output_format=output_format,
                output_config=cast(Any, {"effort": effort or self.effort}),
                betas=[FALLBACK_BETA],
                fallbacks="default",
            )
        except anthropic.APITimeoutError as exc:
            return done(Status.TIMEOUT, str(exc))
        except anthropic.APIError as exc:  # status, rate-limit and connection errors alike: HOLD
            return done(Status.ERROR, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
        usage = getattr(response, "usage", None)
        tokens_in = int(getattr(usage, "input_tokens", 0) or 0)
        tokens_out = int(getattr(usage, "output_tokens", 0) or 0)
        write = int(getattr(usage, "cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) or 0)
        read = int(getattr(usage, "cache_read_input_tokens", 0) or 0)
        used = str(getattr(response, "model", model or self.model))
        spend: dict[str, Any] = {
            "model": used,
            "input_tokens": tokens_in,
            "output_tokens": tokens_out,
            "cache_write_tokens": write,
            "cache_read_tokens": read,
            "cost_usd": cost_usd(used, tokens_in, tokens_out, write, read),
        }
        stop = getattr(response, "stop_reason", None)
        if stop == "refusal":
            details = getattr(response, "stop_details", None)
            category = getattr(details, "category", None) if details is not None else None
            return done(Status.REFUSED, f"refusal ({category})", **spend)
        if stop == "max_tokens":
            return done(Status.TRUNCATED, "max_tokens", **spend)
        parsed = getattr(response, "parsed_output", None)
        if parsed is None:
            return done(Status.INVALID, "no parsed answer", **spend)
        return done(Status.OK, parsed=parsed, **spend)


def assess_with(provider: AIProvider, request: AssessmentInput) -> Assessment:
    """The entry review through any provider's structured call (the Anthropic API, the Codex CLI): the
    answer checked against the request (:func:`validate`)."""
    answer = provider.ask(request.signal_key, SYSTEM_PROMPT, request.to_json(), AssessmentV1)
    spend: dict[str, Any] = {
        "model": answer.model,
        "input_tokens": answer.input_tokens,
        "output_tokens": answer.output_tokens,
        "cost_usd": answer.cost_usd,
        "latency_ms": answer.latency_ms,
        "created_at": answer.created_at,
    }
    if answer.status is not Status.OK:
        return Assessment(answer.status, request.signal_key, detail=answer.detail, **spend)
    try:
        checked = validate(answer.parsed, request)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return Assessment(Status.INVALID, request.signal_key, detail=str(exc)[:500], **spend)
    return Assessment(
        Status.OK,
        request.signal_key,
        verdict=checked.verdict,
        confidence=checked.confidence,
        reasons=tuple(checked.reasons),
        **spend,
    )
            return done(Status.TRUNCATED, "max_tokens", **spend)
        parsed = getattr(response, "parsed_output", None)
        if parsed is None:
            return done(Status.INVALID, "no parsed answer", **spend)
        return done(Status.OK, parsed=parsed, **spend)


def assess_with(provider: AIProvider, request: AssessmentInput) -> Assessment:
    """The entry review through any provider's structured call (the Anthropic API, the Codex CLI): the
    answer checked against the request (:func:`validate`)."""
    answer = provider.ask(request.signal_key, SYSTEM_PROMPT, request.to_json(), AssessmentV1)
    spend: dict[str, Any] = {
        "model": answer.model,
        "input_tokens": answer.input_tokens,
        "output_tokens": answer.output_tokens,
        "cost_usd": answer.cost_usd,
        "latency_ms": answer.latency_ms,
        "created_at": answer.created_at,
    }
    if answer.status is not Status.OK:
        return Assessment(answer.status, request.signal_key, detail=answer.detail, **spend)
    try:
        checked = validate(answer.parsed, request)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return Assessment(Status.INVALID, request.signal_key, detail=str(exc)[:500], **spend)
    return Assessment(
        Status.OK,
        request.signal_key,
        verdict=checked.verdict,
        confidence=checked.confidence,
        reasons=tuple(checked.reasons),
        **spend,
    )

codex
# Find what the AI lane swallows

**Read-only report.** I did not write `SWALLOWED.md` or change any files. I found 19 `except` handlers in `app/ai/`; there are **none** in `app/engine/ai_trader.py` or `app/strategy/ai_lane.py`.

## The swallower

The exception is caught at **`app/ai/watcher.py:226`**.

The path is:

1. `AITraderLane._decide()` passes the contents of `suspended.json` to `trader.decide()` (`app/engine/ai_trader.py:191–203`).
2. `trader.decide()` calls `suspended()` before it prompts the provider or writes a decision (`app/ai/trader.py:402`).
3. `suspended()` catches `ValueError`/`TypeError` from `pd.Timestamp(until)` and raises a clearer `ValueError` (`app/ai/trader.py:243–252`).
4. `AIWatcher.on_bar()` catches that exception, logs it, appends a `FAILED` line to `watch_alerts.log`, then returns `None` (`app/ai/watcher.py:226–231`). The replay can continue, but no decision or session-journal entry is written for that wake.

**Proof in the replay artifacts:** `watch_alerts.log` contains repeated `FAILED` lines with `DateParseError: Unknown datetime string format` and the prose suspension note. Its tail shows failures numbered 1365–1370. This confirms the exception was caught and the replay continued. It also means the brief’s “no log line” is not true of the artifacts I inspected: the error is in `watch_alerts.log`, though it is absent from the normal decision/session journal.

The current `suspended.json` contains a valid timestamp, so it does not reproduce the bad input as it stands. The alert log preserves evidence of the malformed value that triggered those failures.

## Exception inventory

**SAFE** means the failure or invalid result is reported through a status, log, alert file, raised error, or stored row. **SILENT** means the handler discards the exception without an error record. “Journal” below means the replay decision/session journal unless a different store is named.

| Handler | What can reach it; what happens next | Journal visibility | Classification |
|---|---|---|---|
| `app/ai/advisory.py:141` | `_num()` receives a value `float()` cannot convert. Returns `None` without logging. | No error marker; a downstream reader may see a missing value. | **SILENT** |
| `app/ai/advisory.py:290` | Provider output fails Pydantic validation. Converts it to a descriptive `ValueError` and raises it. The notes caller records `INVALID`. | Not the trade journal; the advisory note row records status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/notes.py:113` | An unexpected exception escapes `_call()` in synchronous mode. `_failed()` increments stats and logs an error; it does not create a note row for that exception. | No replay decision entry; system log reports it. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/notes.py:202` | `check()` rejects a nominally successful answer. Converts it to `Status.INVALID`; `_call()` stores that status and detail in `AINoteRow`. | Not the trade journal; visible in the AI notes record. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/providers.py:199` | Anthropic package is unavailable. Returns `Status.ERROR` through `done()`, which logs the result. | Downstream decision or note consumer receives the error status. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/providers.py:217` | Anthropic API timeout. Returns `Status.TIMEOUT` with detail and logs via `done()`. | Downstream record can show timeout status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/providers.py:219` | Other Anthropic API errors, including status, rate-limit, and connection errors. Returns `Status.ERROR` with type and detail; logs via `done()`. | Downstream record can show error status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/providers.py:264` | Answer validation fails after a nominal `OK`. Returns `Assessment(Status.INVALID, detail=...)`. | Not necessarily the replay decision journal; the assessment consumer receives an explicit invalid status. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:231` | `subprocess.run()` times out for the JSON-mode replay provider. Returns `Answer(Status.TIMEOUT, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show timeout. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:233` | Starting that provider raises `OSError`. Returns `Answer(Status.ERROR, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show error and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:299` | Codex CLI subprocess times out. Returns `Answer(Status.TIMEOUT, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show timeout. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:301` | Starting the Codex CLI raises `OSError`. Returns `Answer(Status.ERROR, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show error and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:384` | A line in Codex’s JSON event stream is malformed. Skips the line and continues without recording it. | The journal may show a final answer but not that an event line was discarded. | **SILENT** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:406` | Provider output is not valid JSON. Returns `Answer(Status.ERROR, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show error and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:410` | Parsed JSON fails output-schema validation. Returns `Answer(Status.INVALID, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show invalid status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:422` | CLI stdout is not valid JSON. Returns `Answer(Status.ERROR, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show error and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/replay.py:446` | CLI structured output fails schema validation. Returns `Answer(Status.INVALID, detail=...)`. | Downstream AI record can show invalid status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/schema.py:144` | Assessment output fails Pydantic validation. Raises a descriptive `ValueError`; `assess_with()` converts it to an invalid assessment. | Consumer receives an invalid status and detail. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/trader.py:245` | A suspension value cannot be parsed as a timestamp. Raises a descriptive `ValueError` instead of treating the rule as suspended. The watcher catches it at line 226. | No decision/session-journal entry for the failed wake; `watch_alerts.log` records it. | **SAFE** |
| `app/ai/watcher.py:226` | Any exception from a watcher cycle, including the suspension error. Increments failures, logs a traceback, appends to `watch_alerts.log`, and returns `None`. | No decision/session-journal entry for that wake; alert log reports it. | **SAFE** |

## What a journal reader could wrongly conclude

- **`advisory.py:141` — SILENT:** A nonnumeric input becomes `None` with no explanation. A reader could take the resulting absence of a value as ordinary missing data rather than a conversion failure.
- **`replay.py:384` — SILENT:** Malformed Codex event lines disappear. A reader could assume the event stream was complete; the journal only reflects whatever valid events and final answer survived parsing.

For the suspension failure, a reader looking only at the normal journal could conclude that the replay simply had no decision at that time—or that no wake was needed. The separate alert log shows that a wake failed before a decision was produced.

## The future watcher state

This incident is a silent wake-condition failure, not an `except` handler. `wake_reasons()` computes minutes since `last_call`; when that timestamp is in the future, the value is negative. Cooldown, behaviour, plan-level, and heartbeat checks then fail their `minutes >= …` conditions, so the watcher can produce no wakes. The replay journal can consequently report “AI woken 0 times” without revealing the bad state.

`research/2026-10-24-replay/day.py` now has `check_watcher_is_not_ahead()` to stop a replay whose stored `last_call` is later than the requested day. The current `watch_state.json` is for 2025-02-26; the stored state no longer shows the future timestamp described in the brief.

## One change that would have exposed today’s failure

In `AIWatcher.on_bar()`’s exception handler, **append a `FAILED` record containing `as_of` and the exception detail to the same decision/session journal used for successful wakes before returning `None`**. That would make a failed wake visible to the replay report reader without changing the broader flow.

tokens used
